															else

																else
																	color4 = color2
																end
															end

															local v111 = tbl11.eggSub(displayName2, color2, n37, str9, n38, v105 and magnitude or nil)

																pcall(setThreadIdentity, 7)
															end

																	pcall(function()
																		v112:Destroy()
																	end)
																end

																tbl11.mkEggEsp(child, isBasePart, str11, v111, color4, color3)
															else

																tbl11.espSet(v112, str11, v111, color4, color3)
															end
														end
													end
												end
											end

											if type(records) == "table" then

																		end

																if eggActive then

																		tbl11.espSet(v112, str13, v111, color4, color3)

														end)

											uid = v106.Uid,
											name = str8,
											base = displayName2,
											rank = n35,
											rar = displayName,
											col = color2,
											price = n36,
											pos = position,
											area = v106.AreaId,
										})
									end
								end

								return tbl30
							end

							tbl11.rarestNow = function()
								local v105 = nil

								for _, v106 in ipairs(tbl11.fieldEggs()) do
									if not v105 or v106.rank > v105.rank or v106.rank == v105.rank and v106.price > v105.price then
										v105 = v106
									end
								end

								return v105
							end

							tbl11.rmRare = function()
								if tbl11.rareCfg.thread then
									task.cancel(tbl11.rareCfg.thread)
									tbl11.rareCfg.thread = nil
								end
							end

												end
											end
										end

										for k in pairs(tbl11.rareCfg.seen) do
											if not tbl30[k] then
												tbl11.rareCfg.seen[k] = nil
											end
										end

										task.wait(2)
										flag25 = false
									end
								end)
							end

								"Rare",

								local tbl30 = {}

								for _, v105 in ipairs(tbl11.fieldEggs()) do
									local base = v105.base or v105.name

									if tbl30[base] == nil or v105.price > tbl30[base] then
										tbl30[base] = v105.price
									end
								end

								local tbl31 = {}

								for k in pairs(tbl30) do
									table.insert(tbl31, k)
								end

									return tbl30[arg] > tbl30[arg2]
								end)

							end

							tbl11.rarName = function(arg)
								for k, v105 in pairs(tbl11.rarRank) do

							tbl11.hop = function()
								if tbl11.huntCfg.hopping then
									return v85[132]
								end
								tbl11.huntCfg.hopping = true
								local tbl30 = {}
								local nextPageCursor = nil

								for i = 1, 3 do
									local str8 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Desc&excludeFullGames=true&limit=100" .. (nextPageCursor and "&cursor=" .. nextPageCursor or "")

									local ok, result = pcall(function()
										return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(str8))
									end)

									if not (not ok or type(result) ~= "table") then
										local v105 = ipairs
										local data = result.data or {}

										for _, v106 in v105(data) do
											local flag25 = v106.id ~= game.JobId

											if flag25 then
												flag25 = (tonumber(v106.playing) or 0) < (tonumber(v106.maxPlayers) or v85[138])
											end

											if flag25 then
												table.insert(tbl30, v106.id)
											end
										end

										nextPageCursor = result.nextPageCursor
										if not (not nextPageCursor or #tbl30 >= 40) then
											continue
										end
									end

									break
								end

								if #tbl30 == 0 then
									tbl11.huntCfg.hopping = false
									return false
								end
								local queueOnTeleport = queue_on_teleport or queueonteleport or syn and syn.queue_on_teleport

								if queueOnTeleport then
									pcall(queueOnTeleport, "repeat task.wait() until game:IsLoaded()\n" .. (tbl11.huntCfg.on and string.format("getgenv().sae_hunt = { min = %d, hops = %d }\n", tbl11.huntCfg.min, tbl11.huntCfg.hops + v85[41]) or "") .. "loadstring(game:HttpGet(\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nismovxa/acro-hub/refs/heads/main/acro-main\"))()")
								end

								local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")

								if not pcall(function()
									TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, tbl30[math.random(1, #tbl30)], localPlayer)
								end) then
									pcall(function()
										TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, localPlayer)
									end)
								end

								task.delay(15, function()
									tbl11.huntCfg.hopping = false
								end)

								return true
							end

							tbl11.rmHunt = function()
								if tbl11.huntCfg.thread then
									task.cancel(tbl11.huntCfg.thread)

									local now2 = os.clock()

										local v106 = nil

											end

											if flag25 then
												v106 = v107
											end
										end

										if v106 then
											tbl11.huntCfg.on = false
											tbl11.huntCfg.thread = nil

												pcall(setThreadIdentity, v85[174])
											end

											local format = string.format
											local name = v106.name
											local rar = v106.rar
											local str8 = tostring(v106.area or "the field")
											local v108 = v85[175]

											if tbl11.huntT then
												tbl11.huntT.set(false)
											end

										local flag26

										end

											else

												task.wait(5)
											end
										else
											tbl11.huntCfg.status = "Scanning this server..."
											task.wait(1)
										end
									end

									tbl11.huntCfg.thread = nil
								end)
							end

							Main = fn58("Main", "crosshair")
							Premium = fn58("Premium", "package")

								v105:toggle("esp_eggs", "ESP Eggs", false, tbl11.setEggEsp)

						end)

					end

					while true do

			end
		end
	end
